3541 — dumbbell incline y-raise

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3541 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A dumbbell raise on an incline bench, lifting the arms into a Y shape. It trains the lower traps and upper back.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3541.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3541.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

For an otherwise unspecified seated setup, place the feet in a stable comfortable support position; preserve any source-defined footrests, pedals, leg pads or floor contact.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3541.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench, Incline bench configuration. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3541.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards. 3541.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean forward slightly and let your arms hang straight down, keeping a slight bend in your elbows. 3541.json#/instructions/1
  3. Raise your arms out to the sides and up in a Y shape until they are parallel to the ground. 3541.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pause for a moment at the top, then slowly lower your arms back down to the starting position. 3541.json#/instructions/3
  5. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3541.json#/instructions/4

A dumbbell raise on an incline bench, lifting the arms into a T shape. It trains the rear delts and upper back.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Set an incline bench to a 45-degree angle and sit on it with a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards. 3542.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean forward and let your arms hang straight down, perpendicular to the floor. 3542.json#/instructions/1

3543 — bodyweight drop jump squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3543 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat that ends with an explosive jump, bringing the feet together in the air. It builds leg power and cardio.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3543.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Let the upper arms rest near the torso, elbows soft and hands relaxed; allow small balance movements that do not add a new exercise action.

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial arm position was not explicit; this is a neutral staging choice compatible with the inferred support pose. 3543.json#/instructions/0

Legs Feet

Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3543.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3543.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart. 3543.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lower your body into a squat position by bending your knees and pushing your hips back. 3543.json#/instructions/1
  3. Jump up explosively, extending your hips, knees, and ankles. 3543.json#/instructions/2
  4. While in mid-air, quickly bring your feet together. 3543.json#/instructions/3
  5. Land softly on the balls of your feet and immediately drop back into a squat position. 3543.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3543.json#/instructions/5

3544 — bodyweight incline side plank

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3544 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: side_lying.

A side plank held on an incline, resting on the forearm with the body in a straight line. It trains the obliques and core.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Start by lying on your side with your legs extended and stacked on top of each other. Place your forearm on the ground directly below your shoulder, with your elbow bent at a 90-degree angle.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3544.json#/instructions/0, 3544.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Place your forearm on the ground directly below your shoulder, with your elbow bent at a 90-degree angle.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3544.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3544.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: no additional prop is established. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3544.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start by lying on your side with your legs extended and stacked on top of each other. 3544.json#/instructions/0
  2. Place your forearm on the ground directly below your shoulder, with your elbow bent at a 90-degree angle. 3544.json#/instructions/1
  3. Engage your core and lift your hips off the ground, creating a straight line from your head to your feet. 3544.json#/instructions/2
  4. Hold this position for the desired amount of time. 3544.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower your hips back down to the ground and repeat on the other side. 3544.json#/instructions/4

3545 — dumbbell incline alternate press

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3545 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A dumbbell press on an incline bench, with the arms alternating. It trains the upper chest and shoulders.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3545.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3545.json#/instructions/0

authored_from_source_posture; source verified: false. Initial leg placement was not explicit; this is a reversible balance/support choice, not a replacement for source movement. 3545.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench, Incline bench configuration. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3545.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on an incline bench with a dumbbell in each hand, resting on your thighs. 3545.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lean back on the bench and use your thighs to help raise the dumbbells to shoulder height, palms facing forward. 3545.json#/instructions/1
  3. Once at shoulder height, rotate your wrists so that the palms of your hands are facing forward. 3545.json#/instructions/2
  4. Push the dumbbells up with your chest and shoulders, extending your arms fully. 3545.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lower the dumbbells back down to the starting position, keeping your elbows slightly bent. 3545.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions, alternating arms. 3545.json#/instructions/5

3546 — dumbbell seated alternate shoulder

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3546 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: seated.

A seated dumbbell shoulder press with the arms alternating. It trains the delts and triceps.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Sit on a bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. Hold a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards, and raise them to shoulder height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3546.json#/instructions/0, 3546.json#/instructions/1

Arms Hands

Hold a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards, and raise them to shoulder height.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3546.json#/instructions/1

Legs Feet

Sit on a bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 3546.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Dumbbell pair, Adjustable utility bench. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3546.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Sit on a bench with your back straight and feet flat on the ground. 3546.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hold a dumbbell in each hand, palms facing inwards, and raise them to shoulder height. 3546.json#/instructions/1
  3. Press one dumbbell up overhead while keeping the other dumbbell at shoulder height. 3546.json#/instructions/2
  4. Lower the raised dumbbell back to shoulder height while simultaneously pressing the other dumbbell up overhead. 3546.json#/instructions/3
  5. Continue alternating between arms for the desired number of repetitions. 3546.json#/instructions/4

  2. Keep your elbows close to your torso and your back straight. 3560.json#/instructions/1

  4. Continue to raise the dumbbell until your biceps are fully contracted and the dumbbell is at shoulder level. 3560.json#/instructions/3
  5. Inhale and slowly lower the dumbbell back to the starting position. 3560.json#/instructions/4

  8. Extend your arm fully and hold for a moment at the top. 3560.json#/instructions/7
  9. Inhale and slowly lower the dumbbell back to the starting position. 3560.json#/instructions/8
  10. Repeat the press with your left hand. 3560.json#/instructions/9
  11. Continue alternating between curls and presses for the desired number of repetitions. 3560.json#/instructions/10

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 3662 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: plank.

A push-up flow that moves from pike position down and through to a cobra. It trains the shoulders, chest and spine mobility.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Use a high-plank blocking pose on the documented support, with a long aligned torso; preserve any source-defined low-plank or lifted-leg modification.

authored_from_source_context; source verified: false. Body-pose class uses cited setup/action or title/description context, or an explicitly authored neutral default; detailed placement is a reversible proposal. Basis: source_classification. 3662.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 3662.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Start in a push-up position with your hands slightly wider than shoulder-width apart and your feet together. 3662.json#/instructions/0
  2. Engage your core and lift your hips up towards the ceiling, forming an inverted V shape with your body. 3662.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower your upper body towards the ground by bending your elbows, keeping them close to your body. 3662.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you lower down, shift your weight forward and transition into a cobra pose by straightening your arms and lifting your chest up. 3662.json#/instructions/3
  5. Reverse the movement by bending your elbows and lowering your chest back down towards the ground. 3662.json#/instructions/4
  6. Push through your hands to return to the inverted V position. 3662.json#/instructions/5
  7. Continue the movement by lowering your hips back down towards the ground, returning to the starting push-up position. 3662.json#/instructions/6
  8. Repeat for the desired number of repetitions. 3662.json#/instructions/7

A side-lying leg exercise that targets the inner thighs. It trains the adductors and hip stability.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with your feet shoulder-width apart and your knees slightly bent. 3669.json#/instructions/0
  2. Extend your arms straight out in front of you at shoulder height, parallel to the ground. 3669.json#/instructions/1
  3. Rotate your torso to the right, keeping your arms extended and your back straight. 3669.json#/instructions/2
  4. As you rotate, extend your right arm forward and your left arm back, mimicking the motion of drawing a bowstring. 3669.json#/instructions/3
  5. Hold the position for a moment, then return to the starting position. 3669.json#/instructions/4
  6. Repeat the motion, this time rotating your torso to the left and extending your left arm forward and your right arm back. 3669.json#/instructions/5
  7. Continue alternating sides for the desired number of repetitions. 3669.json#/instructions/6
